### [1] [GLEAN: Grounded Lightweight Evaluation Anchors for Contamination-Aware Tabular Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02212)
*Qizhi Wang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: GLEAN是一个轻量级表格推理评估协议，通过污染感知探针、弱监督治理、检索-推理诊断和结构化错误归因，在16GB GPU预算下评估小型模型在表格推理任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格推理基准测试存在语义推理、数值计算和脆弱的表格格式混合问题，且小型模型的评估容易受到数据污染、数据集伪影和检索失败的影响。

Method: 提出GLEAN评估协议，整合以下四个核心组件：1)污染感知探针(contamination-aware probes)；2)弱监督治理(weak-supervision governance)；3)检索-推理诊断(retrieval-reasoning diagnostics)；4)结构化错误归因(structured error attribution)。使用Squall gold SQL作为可执行锚点(95.2%执行率)，建立确定性错误分类体系(L0-L4及L0.5上下文缺失)。

Result: 1)验证了TAPEX错误偏向于接地问题(L3)，而TAPAS错误偏向于幻觉/弃权(L2/L0)，呈现稳定的错误模式分离；2)证据行启发式方法达到0.62精确率/0.71召回率，混合召回率达0.81；3)发现检索Recall@K可能饱和而端到端EM/F1仍然受限，这推动了对原始召回率之外进行归因的必要性。评估数据集包括TabFact、WTQ(via Squall)、TableBench、RobuT和SciTab。

Conclusion: GLEAN作为一个模块化框架，通过审计和敏感性检查，使小型模型的表格推理评估更加污染感知化和诊断化，为表格推理评估提供了新的方法论。

Abstract: Tabular reasoning benchmarks mix semantic inference, numerical computation, and brittle table formatting, yet evaluations for small models remain vulnerable to contamination, dataset artifacts, and retrieval failures. We propose GLEAN, a lightweight evaluation protocol that integrates contamination-aware probes, weak-supervision governance, retrieval-reasoning diagnostics, and structured error attribution under tight hardware constraints. We evaluate across TabFact, WTQ via Squall, TableBench, RobuT, and SciTab under a 16GB GPU budget. Using Squall gold SQL as an executable anchor (95.2% execution), GLEAN assigns a deterministic error taxonomy (L0-L4 plus L0.5 context miss) and reveals a stable error-mode separation: TAPEX errors skew toward grounding (L3) while TAPAS errors skew toward hallucination/abstention (L2/L0). We validate evidence-row heuristics against SQL-derived rows on simple queries (0.62 precision / 0.71 recall; hybrid recall 0.81) and show that retrieval Recall@K can saturate even when end-to-end EM/F1 remains limited, motivating attribution beyond raw recall. We release a modular framework with audits and sensitivity checks to make small-model tabular evaluation more contamination-aware and diagnostic.

</details>


### [2] [HELIOS: Harmonizing Early Fusion, Late Fusion, and LLM Reasoning for Multi-Granular Table-Text Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02248)
*Sungho Park,Joohyung Yun,Jongwuk Lee,Wook-Shin Han*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: HELIOS是一种结合早融合和晚融合优点的表-文本检索方法，通过边-based二分子图检索、查询相关节点扩展和基于星的LLM精炼三个阶段，在OTT-QA基准上实现了显著的性能提升（召回率提升42.6%，nDCG提升39.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究使用早融合或晚融合方法，但存在局限性：早融合预先对齐表行与段落形成"星形"结构，常包含无关上下文且丢失查询相关关系；晚融合动态对齐单个节点，但可能遗漏相关上下文；两者在列聚合和多跳推理等高级推理任务上表现不佳。

Method: 提出HELIOS方法，包含三个阶段：（1）边-based二分子图检索：识别表片段与段落之间的细粒度边，有效避免无关上下文；（2）查询相关节点扩展：识别最有希望的节点，动态检索相关边以扩展二分子图，最小化遗漏重要上下文的风险；（3）基于星的LLM精炼：在星图级别而非二分子图级别进行逻辑推理，支持高级推理任务。

Result: 在OTT-QA基准上，HELIOS优于现有最优模型，召回率提升高达42.6%，nDCG提升高达39.9%。

Conclusion: HELIOS成功结合了早融合和晚融合的优点，通过细粒度边检索、动态节点扩展和星图级推理，有效解决了表-文本检索中的上下文相关性和高级推理问题。

Abstract: Table-text retrieval aims to retrieve relevant tables and text to support open-domain question answering. Existing studies use either early or late fusion, but face limitations. Early fusion pre-aligns a table row with its associated passages, forming "stars," which often include irrelevant contexts and miss query-dependent relationships. Late fusion retrieves individual nodes, dynamically aligning them, but it risks missing relevant contexts. Both approaches also struggle with advanced reasoning tasks, such as column-wise aggregation and multi-hop reasoning. To address these issues, we propose HELIOS, which combines the strengths of both approaches. First, the edge-based bipartite subgraph retrieval identifies finer-grained edges between table segments and passages, effectively avoiding the inclusion of irrelevant contexts. Then, the query-relevant node expansion identifies the most promising nodes, dynamically retrieving relevant edges to grow the bipartite subgraph, minimizing the risk of missing important contexts. Lastly, the star-based LLM refinement performs logical inference at the star graph level rather than the bipartite subgraph, supporting advanced reasoning tasks. Experimental results show that HELIOS outperforms state-of-the-art models with a significant improvement up to 42.6\% and 39.9\% in recall and nDCG, respectively, on the OTT-QA benchmark.

</details>


### [3] [Cross-Layer Decision Timing Orchestration in Cost-Based Database Systems: Resolving Structural Temporal Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02253)
*Ilsun Chang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: This paper addresses execution instability in traditional cost-based DBMS caused by timing misalignment between optimization and execution stages. The authors propose a cross-layer decision timing orchestration framework with a Unified Risk Signal (URS) that shifts decision authority from compile-time optimizer to runtime executor via selective late binding. Experiments on modified PostgreSQL show up to 20x P99 latency reduction under severe estimation drift.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional cost-based DBMS suffer from execution instability and tail-latency amplification due to a structural timing misalignment between optimization and execution stages. The optimizer relies on historical statistics while the executor observes runtime data distributions, and accelerators impose up-front transfer costs. This temporal asynchrony leads to rigid early-bound decisions that fail under input-scale shifts or stale statistics. The paper argues that decision timing and the availability of runtime signals materially affect robustness under uncertainty, beyond just estimation accuracy and raw execution throughput.

Method: The paper proposes a cross-layer decision timing orchestration framework that shifts final decision authority from the compile-time optimizer to the runtime executor via selective late binding of operator-level choices. A Unified Risk Signal (URS) is introduced to integrate optimizer uncertainty, execution-time observations, and accelerator cost signals without collapsing them into a single static cost model. Experiments are conducted on a modified PostgreSQL prototype using controlled microbenchmarks.

Result: Experiments on a modified PostgreSQL prototype evaluated three scenarios: (i) input-scale shift, (ii) stale-statistics drift, and (iii) GPU offload break-even regimes. The proposed orchestration improves execution stability, reducing P99 latency by up to 20x under severe estimation drift while maintaining comparable median latency.

Conclusion: The proposed cross-layer decision timing orchestration framework successfully improves execution stability, reducing P99 latency by up to 20x under severe estimation drift while maintaining comparable median latency. This is achieved through selective late binding of operator-level choices, moving final decision authority from compile-time optimizer to runtime executor.

Abstract: This paper analyzes execution instability in traditional cost-based database management systems (DBMS) and identifies a structural timing misalignment between optimization and execution stages that contributes to tail-latency amplification. Beyond estimation accuracy and raw execution throughput, we argue that decision timing and the availability of runtime signals materially affect robustness under uncertainty.
  In conventional DBMS architectures, the optimizer relies on historical statistics, the executor observes runtime data distributions and resource states, and accelerators impose up-front transfer costs and amortization constraints. This temporal asynchrony can lead to rigid early-bound decisions that fail under input-scale shifts or stale statistics.
  We propose a cross-layer decision timing orchestration framework that shifts final decision authority from the compile-time optimizer to the runtime executor via selective late binding of operator-level choices. A Unified Risk Signal (URS) integrates optimizer uncertainty, execution-time observations, and accelerator cost signals without collapsing them into a single static cost model.
  Experiments on a modified PostgreSQL prototype evaluate (i) input-scale shift, (ii) stale-statistics drift, and (iii) GPU offload break-even regimes using controlled microbenchmarks. The proposed orchestration improves execution stability, reducing P99 latency by up to 20x under severe estimation drift while maintaining comparable median latency.

</details>


### [4] [Large Language Model-Enhanced Relational Operators: Taxonomy, Benchmark, and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02537)
*Yunxiang Su,Tianjing Zeng,Zhongjun Ding,Yin Lin,Rong Zhu,Zhewei Wei,Bolin Ding,Jingren Zhou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对大语言模型增强的关系运算符(LRO)进行了系统研究，建立了统一分类体系，设计了包含350个查询的综合基准LROBench，并通过实验获得了LRO设计的最佳实践经验，为复杂语义查询处理提供了有效指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型(LLM)的发展，许多研究将LLM集成到关系数据处理任务中，但现有LRO存在定义碎片化、实现策略多样化以及评估基准不足等问题，亟需系统性的研究和评估框架。

Method: 首先建立了统一的LRO分类体系，将现有LRO分为Select、Match、Impute、Cluster和Order五类；其次设计了LROBench基准测试，包含290个单LRO查询和60个多LRO查询，涵盖27个数据库和10多个领域；在多种实现下评估了各个LRO，并从中提炼出实践经验；最后对比了现有多LRO系统与基于最佳实践构建的LRO套件的性能差异。

Result: 建立了统一的LRO分类体系；设计了全面的LROBench基准测试；获得了关于LRO设计选择的实践经验；证明了基于最佳实践的LRO套件在处理复杂语义查询方面的有效性。

Conclusion: 研究开源了LROBench基准数据集和评估代码，为后续研究提供了重要资源。并展望了有前景的未来研究方向，为LRO领域的进一步发展奠定了基础。

Abstract: With the development of large language models (LLMs), numerous studies integrate LLMs through operator-like components to enhance relational data processing tasks, e.g., filters with semantic predicates, knowledge-augmented table imputation, reasoning-driven entity matching and more challenging semantic query processing. These components invoke LLMs while preserving a relational input/output interface, which we refer to as LLM-Enhanced Relational Operators (LROs). From an operator perspective, unfortunately, these existing LROs suffer from fragmented definition, various implementation strategies and inadequate evaluation benchmarks. To this end, in this paper, we first establish a unified LRO taxonomy to align existing LROs, and categorize them into: Select, Match, Impute, Cluster and Order, along with their operands and implementation variants. Second, we design LROBench, a comprehensive benchmark featuring 290 single-LRO queries and 60 multi-LRO queries, spanning 27 databases across more than 10 domains. LROBench covers all operating logics and operand granularities in its single-LRO workload, and provides challenging multi-LRO queries stratified by query complexity. Based on these, we evaluate individual LROs under various implementations, deriving practical insights into LRO design choices and summarizing our empirical best practices. We further compare the end-to-end performance of existing multi-LRO systems against an LRO suite instantiated with these best practices, in order to investigate how to design an effective LRO set for multi-LRO systems targeting complex semantic queries. Last, to facilitate future work, we outline promising future directions and open-source all benchmark data and evaluation code, available at https://github.com/LROBench/LROBench/.

</details>


### [5] [Timehash: Hierarchical Time Indexing for Efficient Business Hours Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02941)
*Jinoh Kim,Jaewon Son*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Timehash是一种新型层次化时间索引算法，在位置搜索服务中实现超过99%的索引大小缩减，同时保持100%精度。该方法使用灵活的多分辨率策略，通过数据驱动方式选择最优层级，实验表明五级层级将每文档索引词减少至5.6个，零误报，从100K到12.6M POIs保持常数成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法存在缺陷：范围过滤查询性能差，而分钟级索引导致索引大小爆炸。位置服务需要按营业时间过滤商家，因此需要一种高效的时间范围过滤方案。

Method: 提出了一种名为Timehash的新型层次化时间索引算法，采用灵活的多分辨率策略和可定制的层级结构。通过对12.6 million条生产环境业务记录分布的实证分析，展示了针对特定数据分布选择最优层级的数据驱动方法。

Result: 五级层级将每文档索引词减少到5.6个（比分钟级索引减少99.1%），零假阳性和零假阴性。扩展性测试确认从100K到12.6M POIs的常数每文档成本，同时支持休息时间和不规则营业时间等复杂场景。

Conclusion: Timehash提供了一种实用的时间过滤解决方案，在保持100%精度的同时实现了显著的索引大小缩减。该方法可广泛应用于搜索系统、电子商务和预订平台中的各种时间过滤问题。

Abstract: Temporal range filtering is a critical operation in large-scale search systems, particularly for location-based services that need to filter businesses by operating hours. Traditional approaches either suffer from poor query performance (scope filtering) or index size explosion (minute-level indexing).
  We present Timehash, a novel hierarchical time indexing algorithm that achieves over 99% reduction in index size compared to minute-level indexing while maintaining 100% precision. Timehash employs a flexible multi-resolution strategy with customizable hierarchical levels. Through empirical analysis on distributions from 12.6 million business records of a production location search service, we demonstrate a data-driven methodology for selecting optimal hierarchies tailored to specific data distributions.
  We evaluated Timehash on up to 12.6 million synthetic POIs generated from production distributions. Experimental results show that a five-level hierarchy reduces index terms to 5.6 per document (99.1% reduction versus minute-level indexing), with zero false positives and zero false negatives. Scalability benchmarks confirm constant per-document cost from 100K to 12.6M POIs, while supporting complex scenarios such as break times and irregular schedules. Our approach is generalizable to various temporal filtering problems in search systems, e-commerce, and reservation platforms.

</details>


### [6] [A Graph-Native Approach to Normalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02995)
*Johannes Schrott,Maxime Jakubowski,Katja Hose*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文提出图原生规范化(graph-native normalization)方法，用于解决知识图谱中因缺乏严格模式导致的冗余、不一致和异常问题。该方法考虑了节点内、边及其组合的依赖关系，定义了图原生范式和图对象函数依赖，并提供了转换算法。实验在合成和原生图数据集上进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱(KGs)尤其是标记属性图(LPGs)在众多应用中成为重要组件。尽管KGs缺乏严格模式会导致结构问题，进而引发冗余、不一致和异常，但图谱质量问题至今未得到充分关注。现有研究仅利用节点内依赖性，而现实世界的KGs还存在涉及边的函数依赖。

Method: 提出图原生规范化方法，综合考虑节点内、边以及节点与边组合的依赖关系。定义了多种图原生范式和图对象函数依赖(Graph Object Functional Dependencies)，并设计了相应的图转换算法。

Result: 在多种合成和原生图数据集上验证了所提方法的有效性（具体数值结果摘要未详述）。

Conclusion: 图原生规范化能够有效处理知识图谱中的冗余、不一致和异常问题，为图数据的规范化理论奠定了基础。

Abstract: In recent years, knowledge graphs (KGs) - in particular in the form of labeled property graphs (LPGs) - have become essential components in a broad range of applications. Although the absence of strict schemas for KGs facilitates structural issues that lead to redundancies and subsequently to inconsistencies and anomalies, the problem of KG quality has so far received only little attention. Inspired by normalization using functional dependencies for relational data, a first approach exploiting dependencies within nodes has been proposed. However, real-world KGs also expose functional dependencies involving edges. In this paper, we therefore propose graph-native normalization, which considers dependencies within nodes, edges, and their combination. We define a range of graph-native normal forms and graph object functional dependencies and propose algorithms for transforming graphs accordingly. We evaluate our contributions using a broad range of synthetic and native graph datasets.

</details>


### [7] [V3DB: Audit-on-Demand Zero-Knowledge Proofs for Verifiable Vector Search over Committed Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03065)
*Zipeng Qiu,Wenjie Qu,Jiaheng Zhang,Binhang Yuan*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: V3DB是一个可验证的版本化向量搜索服务，通过零知识证明实现ANN检索结果的可审计性，基于Plonky2实现，比纯电路方案快22倍，内存降低40%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索服务（如语义搜索、推荐系统、RAG）通常只返回top-k结果，缺乏可审计的证据来验证结果正确性。用户无法验证返回结果是否真正由指定算法在承诺的语料库上执行产生。

Method: 将IVF-PQ搜索管道标准化为固定形状的五步查询语义；结合多重集相等/包含性检查与轻量级边界条件，避免代价高昂的电路内排序和随机访问；基于Plonky2零知识证明框架实现。

Result: 原型实现比纯电路基线快达22倍，峰值内存降低达40%，验证时间达到毫秒级。

Conclusion: V3DB首次实现了对近似最近邻检索的可验证性，支持按需审计，无需透露嵌入向量或私有索引内容，为不可信的向量搜索服务提供商提供了可验证性保障。

Abstract: Dense retrieval services increasingly underpin semantic search, recommendation, and retrieval-augmented generation, yet clients typically receive only a top-$k$ list with no auditable evidence of how it was produced. We present V3DB, a verifiable, versioned vector-search service that enables audit-on-demand correctness checks for approximate nearest-neighbour (ANN) retrieval executed by a potentially untrusted service provider. V3DB commits to each corpus snapshot and standardises an IVF-PQ search pipeline into a fixed-shape, five-step query semantics. Given a public snapshot commitment and a query embedding, the service returns the top-$k$ payloads and, when challenged, produces a succinct zero-knowledge proof that the output is exactly the result of executing the published semantics on the committed snapshot -- without revealing the embedding corpus or private index contents. To make proving practical, V3DB avoids costly in-circuit sorting and random access by combining multiset equality/inclusion checks with lightweight boundary conditions. Our prototype implementation based on Plonky2 achieves up to $22\times$ faster proving and up to $40\%$ lower peak memory consumption than the circuit-only baseline, with millisecond-level verification time.
  Github Repo at https://github.com/TabibitoQZP/zk-IVF-PQ.

</details>


### [8] [Virtual-Memory Assisted Buffer Management In Tiered Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03271)
*Yeasir Rayhan,Walid G. Aref*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了vmcache^n，一个支持n层内存（DRAM-RMem-Disk）的虚拟内存辅助缓冲池，利用操作系统页面迁移机制管理多层内存。实验表明vmcache^n在TPC-C工作负载下可实现比vmcache高4倍的查询吞吐量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数据库缓冲管理采用两层架构（DRAM-Disk），但随着远程内存（RMem）等新型内存层级的出现，需要将两层虚拟内存辅助的缓冲管理技术扩展到n层架构，以充分利用不同层级的内存资源。

Method: 提出vmcache^n系统，利用虚拟内存子系统和操作系统调用实现页面在不同内存层级间的迁移。为解决页面迁移瓶颈，引入了move_pages2系统调用，提供细粒度的页面迁移控制能力。

Result: 实验结果表明，vmcache^n在TPC-C工作负载下可实现最高4倍于vmcache的查询吞吐量提升。

Conclusion: 通过将传统两层内存管理扩展到n层内存架构，并利用move_pages2系统调用优化页面迁移过程，vmcache^n能够有效利用多层内存资源，显著提升数据库查询性能。

Abstract: Tiered memory architectures have gained significant traction in the database community in recent years. In these architectures, the on-chip DRAM of the host processor is typically referred to as local memory, and forms the primary tier. Additional byte-addressable, cache-coherent memory resources, collectively referred to as remote memory (RMem, for short), form one or more secondary tiers. RMem is slower than local DRAM but faster than disk, e.g., NUMA memory located on a remote socket, chiplet-attached memory, and memory attached via high-performance interconnect protocols, e.g., RDMA and CXL. In this paper, we discuss how traditional two-tier (DRAM-Disk) virtual-memory assisted Buffer Management techniques generalize to an $n$-tier setting (DRAM-RMem-Disk). We present vmcache$^n$, an $n$-tier virtual-memory-assisted buffer pool that leverages the virtual memory subsystem and operating system calls to migrate pages across memory tiers. In this setup, page migration can become a bottleneck. To address this limitation, we introduce the move_pages2 system call that provides vmcache$^n$ with fine-grained control over the page migration process. Experiments show that vmcache$^n$ can achieve up to 4$\times$ higher query throughput over vmcache for TPC-C workloads.

</details>

### [9] [CUCo: An Agentic Framework for Compute and Communication Co-design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02376)
*Bodun Hu,Yoga Sri Varshan,Saurabh Agarwal,Aditya Akella*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: CUCo是一个无需训练的智能体驱动工作流，可自动生成高性能CUDA内核，实现计算与通信的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动编写同时利用计算和通信的CUDA内核既费时又容易出错，且此前内核优化工作几乎只关注计算部分，通信内核虽占用大量执行时间却被忽视。

Method: 提出CUCo，一个训练-free的智能体驱动工作流，自动生成高性能CUDA内核，对传统上分离的计算和通信进行联合优化。

Result: 超越最先进基线方法，端到端延迟降低最高达1.57倍。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化计算与通信，可解锁现有方法无法实现的新优化机会。

Abstract: Custom CUDA kernel development is essential for maximizing GPU utilization in large-scale distributed LLM training and inference, yet manually writing kernels that jointly leverage both computation and communication remains a labor-intensive and error-prone process. Prior work on kernel optimization has focused almost exclusively on computation, leaving communication kernels largely untouched even though they constitute a significant share of total execution time. We introduce CUCo, a training-free agent-driven workflow that automatically generates high-performance CUDA kernels that jointly orchestrate computation and communication. By co-optimizing these traditionally disjoint components, CUCo unlocks new optimization opportunities unavailable to existing approaches, outperforming state-of-the-art baselines and reducing end-to-end latency by up to $1.57\times$.

</details>


### [10] [Why Atomicity Matters to AI/ML Infrastructure: Snapshots, Firmware Updates, and the Cost of the Forward-In-Time-Only Category Mistake](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02603)
*Paul Borrill*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文识别并形式化了AI/ML训练系统中的FITO范畴错误，证明检查点原子性在异步故障条件下不可达，并提出无需原子快照的收敛协议解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Large-scale AI/ML训练系统依赖两个很少被审视的假设：(1)检查点代表全局训练状态的原子快照，(2)基础设施更新可以在不引起混合协议集群状态的情况下应用。作者指出这两个假设是更深层结构错误的表现，即"正向时间唯一"(FITO)范畴错误，混淆了协议收敛属性与时间谓词。

Method: 作者采用过程代数框架对检查点执行进行形式化建模，在异步组合与崩溃恢复故障条件下进行数学证明。将检查点不一致性问题在epoch格上重新表述，证明原子性是零测度事件，其补集随独立持久域数量呈指数增长。将混合epoch恢复形式化为优化代数中的类型违规。

Result: 证明在异步组合与崩溃恢复故障条件下，任何时间点都不能作为原子性边界。原子性是零测度事件，其补集随独立持久域数量指数增长。原子部署不仅需要共识，还需要epoch转换的共同知识，这在异步系统中不可达。

Conclusion: 提出了一种受Open Atomic Ethernet启发的双边收敛协议，该协议可在不要求Snap(t)的情况下实现Conv(P,e)，用约束语义替代FITO假设。

Abstract: Large-scale AI/ML training systems depend on two assumptions that are rarely examined: (1) that checkpoints represent atomic snapshots of global training state, and (2) that infrastructure updates can be applied without inducing mixed-protocol cluster states. Both assumptions are instances of a deeper structural error: the Forward-In-Time-Only (FITO) category mistake, which confuses protocol convergence properties with temporal predicates.
  We formalize this confusion as a type error: the identification of a temporal snapshot $\mathsf{Snap}(t)$ with a convergence property $\mathsf{Conv}(\mathcal{P},e)$. We model checkpoint execution in a process-algebraic framework and prove that under asynchronous composition with crash-recovery failures, no temporal instant can serve as an atomicity boundary. We reformulate checkpoint inconsistency on an epoch lattice and show that atomicity is a measure-zero event whose complement grows exponentially with the number of independent persistence domains. We formalize mixed-epoch recovery as a type violation in the optimization algebra and show that the resulting update is not a valid step of any standard optimizer.
  For firmware fleet updates, we strengthen the known consensus-hardness result: atomic deployment requires not merely agreement but common knowledge of the epoch transition, which is strictly unattainable in asynchronous systems with unreliable communication.
  We conclude by sketching a bilateral convergence protocol, inspired by Open Atomic Ethernet, that achieves $\mathsf{Conv}(\mathcal{P},e)$ without requiring $\mathsf{Snap}(t)$ -- replacing the FITO assumption with constraint semantics.

</details>


### [11] [Undecided State Dynamics with Many Opinions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02636)
*Colin Cooper,Frederik Mallmann-Trenn,Tomasz Radzik,Nobutaka Shimizu,Takeharu Shiraga*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了Undecided-State Dynamics (USD)，这是一种基本的一致性过程，其中每个顶点持有k个已决定意见之一或未决定状态。作者在gossip模型和population protocol模型中获得了第一个对任意2≤k≤n和任意初始配置都成立的一致性时间保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的工作仅在限制性条件下建立了USD共识时间的紧边界：对于population protocol模型仅在k = O(√n/(log n)²)的情况下，对于gossip模型仅在k = O((n/log n)^{1/3})的情况下，且通常对初始配置有严格假设。因此，需要在任意k值和任意初始配置下获得一致性时间保证。

Method: 作者分析了USD在两种模型下的行为：gossip模型（同步轮次）和population protocol模型（异步交互）。

Result: 在gossip模型中，USD在~O(min{k, √n})同步轮次内达到一致性，概率为1-p_⊥-n^{-c}，其中p_⊥是gossip模型中第一轮塌缩到全未决定状态的概率。在population protocol模型中，USD在~O(min{k n, n^{3/2})异步交互内以高概率达到一致性。作者还展示了与上界在多对数因子范围内匹配的下界，并表明上界本质上是最佳的。

Conclusion: 本文填补了USD分析中的关键空白，首次为任意k值和任意初始配置提供了一致性时间保证，并证明了这些上界在多对数因子范围内是最优的。

Abstract: We study the Undecided-State Dynamics (USD), a fundamental consensus process in which each vertex holds one of $k$ decided opinions or the undecided state. We consider both the gossip model and the population protocol model. Prior work established tight bounds on the consensus time of this process only for the regime $k = O(\sqrt{n}/(\log n)^2)$ (for the population protocol model) and $k = O((n/\log n)^{1/3})$ (for the gossip model), often under restrictive assumptions on the initial configuration.
  In this paper, we obtain the first consensus-time guarantees for USD that hold for \emph{arbitrary} $2\le k\le n$ and for \emph{arbitrary} initial configurations in both the gossip model and the population protocol model. In the gossip model, USD reaches consensus within $\widetilde O(\min\{k,\sqrt n\})$ synchronous rounds with probability $1-p_{\bot}-n^{-c}$, where $p_{\bot}$ is the gossip-specific probability of collapsing to the all-undecided state in the first round. In the population protocol model, USD reaches consensus within $\widetilde O(\min\{kn,n^{3/2}\})$ asynchronous interactions with high probability. We also present lower bounds that match the upper bounds up to polylogarithmic factors for a specific initial configuration and show that our upper bounds are essentially optimal.

</details>


### [12] [Scalable Mesh Coupling for Atmospheric Wave Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02971)

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文描述了一种可扩展算法，用于在两个求解器的重叠网格区域中插值求解数据，以实现大气波模拟的原地耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大气波模拟中，需要将两个求解器的解进行耦合，以获得全局一致的解。关键挑战在于如何在重叠网格区域高效、准确地插值求解数据。

Method: 采用可扩展的插值算法处理两个求解器重叠网格区域中的解数据耦合。

Result: 提供了算法的时间性能测试数据，并展示了实际应用案例的运行结果。

Conclusion: 该算法可有效实现大气波模拟的原地耦合计算，具有实用价值。

Abstract: We describe the application of a scalable algorithm for interpolating solution data in the overlapping mesh region of two solvers. This feature is essential to obtain a globally consistent solution for in-situ coupled atmospheric wave simulation. We provide timings and discuss a real-world application run.

</details>


### [13] [Dynamic Contract Analysis for Parallel Programming Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03023)
*Yussur Mustafa Oraji,Alexander Hück,Christian Bischof*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出CoVer-Dynamic作为CoVer的动态分析扩展，通过重用契约语言实现统一的静态-动态验证框架，解决静态分析无法处理运行时依赖行为的问题，在MPI和OpenSHMEM基准测试中表现优于MUST工具，平均加速2倍


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析无法完全推理运行时依赖行为（如指针别名或间接控制流），需要动态分析补充；同时现有正确性检查工具仅针对单一编程模型，限制了其适用性

Method: 提出CoVer-Dynamic动态分析扩展，重用CoVer的契约语言，在运行时强制执行相同的契约检查规则，实现静态-动态联合验证

Result: CoVer-Dynamic在标准化MPI和OpenSHMEM基准测试中消除了假阳性，提高了分类准确性，检测到仅静态分析无法发现的错误；平均比MUST快2倍

Conclusion: CoVer-Dynamic成功实现了统一的静态-动态验证框架，通过在运行时强制执行相同的契约，提高了分类准确性并消除了标准化MPI和OpenSHMEM基准测试中的假阳性。该工具平均比MUST快2倍，但当前契约语言的表达能力存在局限，需要进一步扩展以支持更多错误类别。

Abstract: Parallel programming in high-performance computing depends on low-level APIs such as MPI, requiring users to manage synchronization and resources manually. Several correctness checking tools exist to help bug-free code development, though most target a single programming model, limiting their applicability. Our previous work, the static analysis tool CoVer, leverages a contract-based approach enabling users to specify custom error-checking rules and support emerging or unconventional programming models without requiring extensive new tooling. However, static analysis cannot fully reason about runtime-dependent behavior such as pointer aliasing or indirect control flow. To address this, we present CoVer-Dynamic, a dynamic analysis extension that reuses CoVer's contract language to provide a unified static-dynamic verification framework. By enforcing the same contracts at runtime, CoVer-Dynamic improves classification accuracy and eliminates false positives on standardized MPI and OpenSHMEM benchmarks, while detecting errors beyond static analysis only. Our evaluation shows that CoVer-Dynamic consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art correctness checker MUST, averaging a 2x speedup. Finally, our results show limitations in the expressiveness of the contract language, motivating future work to support more error classes.

</details>


### [14] [Serverless Abstractions for Short-Running, Lightweight Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03089)
*Natalie Carl,Niklas Kowallik,Constantin Stahl,Trever Schirmer,Tobias Pfandzelter,David Bermbach*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 论文提出Stream Functions作为一种新型FaaS扩展模型，将短流作为执行、状态和扩展的基本单位，通过迭代器接口实现事件间逻辑处理，兼顾serverless的弹性和零扩展能力。实验表明该方法在视频处理场景中相比成熟流处理引擎减少约99%的处理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Serverless计算和流处理是两种主流的事件驱动数据处理范式，但两者对于需要状态处理的短运行、轻量级和不可预测流的处理效率较低，现有模型无法有效支持这类工作负载。

Method: 提出Stream Functions模型，将短流作为执行、状态和扩展的基本单元。通过迭代器接口处理流，实现无缝的事件间逻辑处理，同时保留serverless平台的弹性和零扩展能力。

Result: 在视频处理用例中，Stream Functions相比成熟的流处理引擎减少约99%的处理开销，同时提供与serverless函数相当的性能并具备流语义。

Conclusion: Stream Functions为现有模型无法有效服务的一类工作负载提供了有效且高效的处理抽象，填补了Serverless计算与流处理之间的空白。

Abstract: Serverless computing and stream processing represent two dominant paradigms for event-driven data processing, yet both make assumptions that render them inefficient for short-running, lightweight, and unpredictable streams that require stateful processing. We propose stream functions as a novel extension of the Function-as-a-Serivce model that treat short streams as the unit of execution, state, and scaling. Stream functions process streams via an iterator-based interface, enabling seamless inter-event logic while retaining the elasticity and scale-to-zero capabilities offered by serverless platforms. Our evaluation shows that stream functions reduce the processing overhead by ~99 % compared to a mature stream process- ing engine in a video-processing use case. By providing comparable performance to serverless functions with stream semantics, stream functions provide an effective and efficient abstractions for a class of workloads underserved by existing models.

</details>

### [15] [The Alignment Flywheel: A Governance-Centric Hybrid MAS for Architecture-Agnostic Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02259)
*Elias Malomgré,Pieter Simoens*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出Alignment Flywheel架构，通过将决策生成与安全治理解耦，利用多智能体系统实现对强大学主但易错的人工智能组件的可审计、可更新的安全治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着日益强大的自主决策组件被嵌入基于智能体的系统，现有的安全行为往往与训练过程纠缠在一起，导致不透明、难以审计且部署后更新成本高昂的问题，需要一种可治理的混合多智能体系统架构。

Method: 设计了一种治理中心的混合MAS架构，包含四个核心组件：1) Proposer（提议者）：代表自主决策组件生成候选轨迹；2) Safety Oracle（安全预言机）：通过稳定接口返回原始安全信号；3. Enforcement layer（执行层）：在运行时应用显式风险策略；4) Governance MAS（治理智能体）：通过审计、不确定性驱动的验证和版本化 refinement 对Oracle进行监督。核心工程原则是"patch locality"（补丁局部性）。

Result: 该架构与Proposer和Safety Oracle的实现无关，指定了运行时门控、审计接收、签名补丁和分布式部署分阶段发布所需的角色、制品、协议和发布语义。

Conclusion: 为将高能力但易错的自主系统集成到显式、版本控制和可审计的监督之下提供了一种混合MAS工程框架。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems provide mature methodologies for role decomposition, coordination, and normative governance, capabilities that remain essential as increasingly powerful autonomous decision components are embedded within agent-based systems. While learned and generative models substantially expand system capability, their safety behavior is often entangled with training, making it opaque, difficult to audit, and costly to update after deployment. This paper formalizes the Alignment Flywheel as a governance-centric hybrid MAS architecture that decouples decision generation from safety governance. A Proposer, representing any autonomous decision component, generates candidate trajectories, while a Safety Oracle returns raw safety signals through a stable interface. An enforcement layer applies explicit risk policy at runtime, and a governance MAS supervises the Oracle through auditing, uncertainty-driven verification, and versioned refinement. The central engineering principle is patch locality: many newly observed safety failures can be mitigated by updating the governed oracle artifact and its release pipeline rather than retracting or retraining the underlying decision component. The architecture is implementation-agnostic with respect to both the Proposer and the Safety Oracle, and specifies the roles, artifacts, protocols, and release semantics needed for runtime gating, audit intake, signed patching, and staged rollout across distributed deployments. The result is a hybrid MAS engineering framework for integrating highly capable but fallible autonomous systems under explicit, version-controlled, and auditable oversight.

</details>


### [16] [StitchCUDA: An Automated Multi-Agents End-to-End GPU Programing Framework with Rubric-based Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02637)
*Shiyang Li,Zijian Zhang,Winson Chen,Yuebo Luo,Mingyi Hong,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: StitchCUDA是一个端到端GPU程序生成的多Agent框架，通过Planner、Coder、Verifier三个专业Agent协作，结合基于规则的正则化强化学习，在KernelBench上达到近100%成功率，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代ML工作负载依赖GPU，但实现端到端高性能仍具挑战；现有LLM方法主要关注单内核优化，未扩展到端到端程序，阻碍实际部署。

Method: 提出StitchCUDA多Agent框架，包含Planner、Coder、Verifier三个专业Agent；集成基于规则的正则化强化学习，聚焦任务到代码生成和反馈驱动代码优化两个原子技能，结合规则奖励和基于真实执行的 rubric 奖励。

Result: 在KernelBench上实现近100%端到端GPU编程任务成功率；比多Agent基线提升1.72x加速比；比RL模型基线提升2.73x加速比。

Conclusion: StitchCUDA通过多Agent协作和基于规则的正则化强化学习，成功实现了端到端GPU程序的高效生成，在KernelBench上达到近100%成功率，分别比多Agent基线和RL模型基线提升1.72x和2.73x加速比。

Abstract: Modern machine learning (ML) workloads increasingly rely on GPUs, yet achieving high end-to-end performance remains challenging due to dependencies on both GPU kernel efficiency and host-side settings. Although LLM-based methods show promise on automated GPU kernel generation, prior works mainly focus on single-kernel optimization and do not extend to end-to-end programs, hindering practical deployment.
  To address the challenge, in this work, we propose StitchCUDA, a multi-agent framework for end-to-end GPU program generation, with three specialized agents: a Planner to orchestrate whole system design, a Coder dedicated to implementing it step-by-step, and a Verifier for correctness check and performance profiling using Nsys/NCU. To fundamentally improve the Coder's ability in end-to-end GPU programming, StitchCUDA integrates rubric-based agentic reinforcement learning over two atomic skills, task-to-code generation and feedback-driven code optimization, with combined rubric reward and rule-based reward from real executions. Therefore, the Coder learns how to implement advanced CUDA programming techniques (e.g., custom kernel fusion, cublas epilogue), and we also effectively prevent Coder's reward hacking (e.g., just copy PyTorch code or hardcoding output) during benchmarking. Experiments on KernelBench show that StitchCUDA achieves nearly 100% success rate on end-to-end GPU programming tasks, with 1.72x better speedup over the multi-agent baseline and 2.73x than the RL model baselines.

</details>


### [17] [Generalized Per-Agent Advantage Estimation for Multi-Agent Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02654)
*Seongmin Kim,Giseung Park,Woojun Kim,Jiwon Jeon,Seungyeol Han,Youngchul Sung*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 一种新型多智能体强化学习框架，利用广义per-agent优势估计器(GPAE)和双截断重要性采样，通过精确优势估计提升样本效率和协同能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在提升多智能体强化学习中的样本效率和协同能力，通过精确的per-agent优势估计来解决现有方法的问题。

Method: 提出广义per-agent优势估计器(GPAE)，采用per-agent价值迭代算子计算精确的per-agent优势，通过动作概率间接估计值以实现稳定的离策略学习；引入双截断重要性采样方案，平衡智能体自身策略变化的敏感性与对其他智能体非平稳性的鲁棒性。

Result: 在基准测试上的实验表明，该方法在协同和复杂场景的样本效率方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的GPAE框架通过精确的per-agent优势估计和双截断重要性采样方案，有效提升了多智能体强化学习中的样本效率和协同能力，在复杂场景中表现优于现有方法。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a novel framework for multi-agent reinforcement learning that enhances sample efficiency and coordination through accurate per-agent advantage estimation. The core of our approach is Generalized Per-Agent Advantage Estimator (GPAE), which employs a per-agent value iteration operator to compute precise per-agent advantages. This operator enables stable off-policy learning by indirectly estimating values via action probabilities, eliminating the need for direct Q-function estimation. To further refine estimation, we introduce a double-truncated importance sampling ratio scheme. This scheme improves credit assignment for off-policy trajectories by balancing sensitivity to the agent's own policy changes with robustness to non-stationarity from other agents. Experiments on benchmarks demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing approaches, excelling in coordination and sample efficiency for complex scenarios.

</details>

### [18] [Fuzzing Microservices in Face of Intrinsic Uncertainties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02551)
*Man Zhang,Tao Yue,Andrea Arcuri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的微服务测试范式：不确定性驱动和系统级测试。现有的微服务测试方法无法有效捕获和传播不确定性，且主要关注单服务测试。文章提出了一种连续不确定性驱动的系统级模糊测试架构，集成了服务虚拟化、不确定性模拟、自适应测试生成和优化，旨在提高工业微服务系统的可靠性和弹性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务的固有特性（如动态可扩展性和去中心化控制）引入了多维度的不确定性，这些不确定性跨越服务间交互、运行时环境和内部服务逻辑，表现为非确定性行为、性能波动和不可预测的故障传播。现有方法不足以捕获这些不确定性及其在工业微服务系统中的传播。

Method: 提出了一种连续不确定性驱动的系统级微服务模糊测试架构，集成四个核心组件：1) 服务虚拟化；2) 不确定性模拟；3) 自适应测试生成；4) 优化。使用电子商务示例进行说明。

Result: 本文主要贡献是提出了新的测试范式和架构框架，识别了三个关键研究挑战：不确定性的建模与注入、不确定性的传播、故障定位的因果推理，以及多维度分析与评估。

Conclusion: 该研究旨在启发开发可扩展的自动化系统级测试方法，明确考虑不确定性及其传播，以提高工业微服务系统的可靠性和弹性。

Abstract: The widespread adoption of microservices has fundamentally transformed how modern software systems are designed, deployed, operated and maintained. However, well-known microservice properties (e.g., dynamic scalability and decentralized control) introduce inherent and multi-dimensional uncertainties. These uncertainties span across inter-service interactions, runtime environments, and internal service logic, which manifest as nondeterministic behaviors, performance fluctuations, and unpredictable fault propagation. Existing approaches do not have sufficient support in capturing such uncertainties and their propagation in industrial microservice systems, and these approaches mostly focus on single-service testing. In this paper, we argue for a novel paradigm: ``uncertainty-driven'' and ``system-level'' microservice testing. We outline key research challenges, including the modeling and injection of uncertainties and their propagation, causal inference for fault localization, and multi-dimensional analyses and assessment of uncertainties and their impact on system quality. We propose an architecture for continuous uncertainty-driven and system-level microservice fuzzing, which integrates service virtualization, uncertainty simulation, adaptive test generation and optimization\revision{, and illustrate it with an e-commerce example we developed}. Our goal is to inspire the development of scalable and automated system-level testing methods that improve the dependability and resilience of industrial microservice systems, with the explicit consideration of uncertainties and their propagation.

</details>


### [19] [His2Trans: A Skeleton First Framework for Self Evolving C to Rust Translation with Historical Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02617)
*Shengbo Wang,Mingwei Liu,Guangsheng Ou,Yuwen Chen,Zike Li,Yanlin Wang,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: His2Trans是一个用于大规模C到Rust自动迁移的框架，通过构建感知骨架图提供严格类型化环境，结合检索增强生成从历史迁移中提取知识，在工业项目中达到99.75%编译通过率，降低23.6%不安全代码，修复效率提升60%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM驱动的C-to-Rust迁移方法在从代码片段扩展到工业级项目时面临系统性障碍，主要源于构建上下文缺失("依赖地狱")和领域特定演化知识缺失，导致无法精确重构复杂构建系统下的类型定义，无法推断惯用API对应关系，从而产生幻觉依赖和无效修复循环。

Method: His2Trans采用双层架构：(1)结构层面通过构建追踪创建可编译的项目级骨架图(PSG)，提供严格类型化环境，将全局验证与局部逻辑生成分离；(2)认知层面从历史迁移痕迹中提取细粒度API和代码片段规则，利用检索增强生成(RAG)系统引导LLM实现惯用接口复用。

Result: 在OpenHarmony工业模块上达到99.75%增量编译通过率，有效修复了基线方法难以解决的构建失败；在通用基准上比C2Rust降低23.6个百分点的不安全代码比例，产生最少警告；知识积累研究表明框架的进化行为使未见任务的修复开销降低约60%。

Conclusion: His2Trans通过结合确定性构建感知骨架与自演化知识提取，成功解决了大规模C到Rust迁移中的核心难题。实验表明该框架在工业级项目(OpenHarmony)中达到99.75%的增量编译通过率，在通用基准上比C2Rust降低23.6个百分点的不安全代码比例，并通过持续集成验证模式将未见任务的修复开销降低约60%，证明了其进化特性。

Abstract: Automated C-to-Rust migration encounters systemic obstacles when scaling from code snippets to industrial projects, mainly because build context is often unavailable ("dependency hell") and domain-specific evolutionary knowledge is missing. As a result, current LLM-based methods frequently cannot reconstruct precise type definitions under complex build systems or infer idiomatic API correspondences, which in turn leads to hallucinated dependencies and unproductive repair loops. To tackle these issues, we introduce His2Trans, a framework that combines a deterministic, build-aware skeleton with self-evolving knowledge extraction to support stable, incremental migration. On the structural side, His2Trans performs build tracing to create a compilable Project-Level Skeleton Graph, providing a strictly typed environment that separates global verification from local logic generation. On the cognitive side, it derives fine-grained API and code-fragment rules from historical migration traces and uses a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system to steer the LLM toward idiomatic interface reuse. Experiments on industrial OpenHarmony modules show that His2Trans reaches a 99.75% incremental compilation pass rate, effectively fixing build failures where baselines struggle. On general-purpose benchmarks, it lowers the unsafe code ratio by 23.6 percentage points compared to C2Rust while producing the fewest warnings. Finally, knowledge accumulation studies demonstrate the framework's evolutionary behavior: by continuously integrating verified patterns, His2Trans cuts repair overhead on unseen tasks by about 60%.

</details>


### [20] [ICSE 2023 Sustainability Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02694)
*Patricia Lago,Marcel Boehme,Markus Funke*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过问卷调查和数据分析评估2023年ICSE会议的碳足迹和参与者满意度，发现航空旅行产生约5053.5吨CO2e，但因响应率低难以得出各活动环节价值的明确结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着学术界对会议碳足迹的讨论增加，需要评估交通等环境因素造成的生态影响是否超过传统论文演示和社交活动的益处。

Method: 对2023年ICSE会议参与者进行问卷调查(161人响应)，收集对不同会议环节的评价;同时根据参会者注册数据估算航空旅行产生的碳足迹。

Result: 会议总体评分4.4/5星，各环节间无显著差异；航空旅行碳足迹约5053.5吨CO2e，响应率仅11%。

Conclusion: 由于响应率较低(11%)，难以得出关于不同会议活动价值的明确结论。会议的航空旅行碳足迹约为5053.5吨CO2e，相当于约1000户家庭一年的用电量。研究为未来会议组织者提供了关于会议地点选择和传统会议活动价值的参考。

Abstract: With growing discussions about the carbon footprint of academic conferences, more questions are being raised whether the environmental impacts caused by transportation and other factors justify the value of traditional paper presentations and social events. There is a pressing need to critically evaluate whether the ecological consequences of these events outweigh their perceived benefits. To that extent, we conducted a questionnaire survey among participants of the 45th International Conference on Software Engineering (ICSE) 2023 in Melbourne, Australia, seeking their feedback on the different conference sessions (e.g., workshops, keynotes, paper presentations, social events). In total, 161 participants filled out our survey. Overall, the conference was rated with 4.4 stars out of 5 stars. We do not see any significant differences among the different sessions, making it difficult to derive conclusions about their certain value and implications to sustainability. The relatively low response rate (11%) did not help in gaining better insights. Based on the participants registration data, we additionally estimated the carbon footprint emerged from air travel. The total carbon dioxide equivalent (CO2e) accumulates to around 5,053.5 tonnes of CO2e which is equivalent to the electricity required to power around 1,000 homes in a year. With this report, we want to provide guidance to organizers of future conference editions with respect to their location and the perceived value of traditional paper presentations, social events, and other sessions.

</details>


### [21] [Reducing Labeling Effort in Architecture Technical Debt Detection through Active Learning and Explainable AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02944)
*Edi Sutoyo,Paris Avgeriou,Andrea Capiluppi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究提出结合关键词过滤、主动学习和可解释AI的方法来减少架构技术债务(ATD)检测中的标注工作量。在103,000个候选问题数据集上，Breaking Ties主动学习策略达到F1=0.72，减少49%标注工作量。SHAP和LIME解释方法中，专家更青睐LIME的清晰度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 架构技术债务(ATD)因其抽象性和上下文依赖性而难以检测，人工标注成本高、耗时长且难以扩展，需要一种方法来减少ATD检测中的标注工作量。

Method: 研究采用三阶段方法：(1) 从57个专家验证的ATD相关Jira问题中提取代表性关键词，用以筛选超过103,000个候选问题；(2) 应用多种查询策略的主动学习框架，优先标注最具信息量的样本；(3) 使用SHAP和LIME两种可解释AI方法对分类结果进行解释和评估。

Result: Breaking Ties策略表现最优，F1分数达到0.72，同时减少了49%的标注工作量。定性评估表明关键词过滤具有可靠性。SHAP和LIME都能提供合理的解释，但专家更偏好LIME的清晰度和易用性。

Conclusion: 研究成功地证明了结合关键词过滤、主动学习和可解释AI在减少ATD检测标注工作量方面的有效性。Breaking Ties策略在F1分数达到0.72的同时减少了49%的标注工作量。SHAP和LIME两种解释方法都能提供合理的模型解释，但专家更倾向于使用LIME，因其更清晰易用。

Abstract: Self-Admitted Technical Debt (SATD) refers to technical compromises explicitly admitted by developers in natural language artifacts such as code comments, commit messages, and issue trackers. Among its types, Architecture Technical Debt (ATD) is particularly difficult to detect due to its abstract and context-dependent nature. Manual annotation of ATD is costly, time-consuming, and challenging to scale. This study focuses on reducing labeling effort in ATD detection by combining keyword-based filtering with active learning and explainable AI. We refined an existing dataset of 116 ATD-related Jira issues from prior work, producing 57 expert-validated items used to extract representative keywords. These were applied to identify over 103,000 candidate issues across ten open-source projects. To assess the reliability of this keyword-based filtering, we conducted a qualitative evaluation of a statistically representative sample of labeled issues. Building on this filtered dataset, we applied active learning with multiple query strategies to prioritize the most informative samples for annotation. Our results show that the Breaking Ties strategy consistently improves model performance, achieving the highest F1-score of 0.72 while reducing the annotation effort by 49\%. In order to enhance model transparency, we applied SHAP and LIME to explain the outcomes of automated ATD classification. Expert evaluation revealed that both LIME and SHAP provided reasonable explanations, with the usefulness of the explanations often depending on the relevance of the highlighted features. Notably, experts preferred LIME overall for its clarity and ease of use.

</details>


### [22] [SEALing the Gap: A Reference Framework for LLM Inference Carbon Estimation via Multi-Benchmark Driven Embodiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02949)
*Priyavanshi Pathania,Rohit Mehra,Vibhu Saujanya Sharma,Vikrant Kaulgud,Tiffani Nevels,Sanjay Podder,Adam P. Burden*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出LLM推理碳排放估计的参考框架SEAL，解决现有方法在提示级别碳测量方面的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型的碳足迹快速增长，虽然训练排放量大，但由于推理处理的提示数量巨大，推理阶段的排放迅速超过训练阶段。因此迫切需要在推理阶段进行准确的提示级别碳测量，以支持可持续性决策。

Method: 提出LLM推理碳估计的指导原则，设计参考框架；实现SEAL工具，采用多基准驱动的方法进行每提示碳估计。

Result: SEAL的初步验证显示出有前景的结果，可作为LLM生态系统可持续性评估标准化的基础。

Conclusion: 提出的框架和SEAL工具为LLM推理碳排放的标准化测量提供了系统基础，有助于推动该领域的可持续性研究。

Abstract: Large Language Models are rapidly gaining traction in software engineering, yet their growing carbon footprint raises pressing sustainability concerns. While training emissions are substantial, inference quickly surpasses them due to the sheer volume of prompts processed. This shift underscores the urgent need for accurate, prompt-level carbon measurement during inference to enable informed, sustainability-focused decision-making. To address the limitations of existing approaches, in this paper, we outline the guiding principles for a novel reference framework for LLM inference carbon estimation that can guide the design of future tools and provide a systematic foundation for advancing sustainability research in this domain. We also introduce SEAL, an early embodiment of these principles that leverages a multi-benchmark-driven approach for per-prompt carbon estimation. Its initial validation shows promising results, positioning SEAL as a foundation for standardized sustainability assessment across the LLM ecosystem.

</details>


### [23] [It's Alive! What a Live Object Environment Changes in Software Engineering Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02987)
*Julián Grigera,Steven Costiou,Juan Cruz Gardey,Stéphane Ducasse*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Pharo Smalltalk环境中的五种开发者工具：Inspector自定义视图、Microcommits、Xtreme TDD、On the Fly重写废弃API以及对象级断点。这些工具体现了Smalltalk的即时反馈和直接对象交互理念，作者借此反思其他IDE应如何改进开发者工作流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发者工具直接影响思维方式。传统IDE中开发者需抽象想象对象状态，而Smalltalk环境让开发者直接与对象交互获得即时反馈。作者认为其他IDE可借鉴这些设计理念来改进开发体验。

Method: 通过展示Pharo中实际使用的五种工具，来说明Smalltalk环境提供的独特开发体验：Inspector自定义视图用于定义特定对象视图和导航；Microcommits支持无需提交即可回退更改；Xtreme TDD允许在调试器中编码；On the Fly重写废弃支持API演进；对象级断点可针对特定实例暂停程序。

Result: 这五种工具体现了Smalltalk的核心设计理念：即时反馈、直接对象交互、灵活可扩展性。这些特性使开发者能更高效地理解和操作程序状态。

Conclusion: 作者邀请其他IDE开发者反思如何借鉴这些Smalltalk特性，重新思考IDE功能设计，以改善开发者的日常工作流程和编程体验。

Abstract: Tools shape our mind. This is why it is important to have extensible and flexible tools for developers to adapt to their needs. Reasoning about programs in the abstract -- by imagining what objects should look like -- can make it harder to grasp the underlying model. In Smalltalk environments like Pharo, developers work closely with their objects, gaining immediate feedback -- not guessing how they will look like but directly interacting with them. This article presents some tools developers use in Pharo: Inspector custom views for defining specific views and navigation for objects, Microcommits for reverting changes without the need to commit and pull, Xtreme TDD that allows developers to code in the debugger, On the Fly Rewriting Deprecations that support API evolution through automated rewriting of deprecated calls, and Object-Centric Breakpoints -- when a problem cannot be efficiently solved with a dummy trace, developers can use break points that will only halt for a given instance. By showcasing these features that evolved alongside Smalltalk, we invite reflection on how other IDEs could rethink some of their features and improve developers' workflows.

</details>


### [24] [A Practical Guide for Establishing a Technical Debt Management Process (Preprint)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03085)
*Marion Wiese,Kamila Serwa,Eva Bittner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 一项关于技术债务管理(TDM)可行性的行动研究，通过5场研讨会和30个月的跟踪调查，识别出TD预防策略和文档的共性，以及优先级和偿还方法的差异，提出了最佳实践建议和研究空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立TD管理流程是实践中最紧迫的问题之一，需要确定先前研究的TDM方法的可行性并识别典型挑战，以创建TDM流程建立指南。

Method: 复制先前发表的行动研究，进行5场TDM介绍研讨会，让两个不同公司的团队决定采用哪些TD活动方法。共进行19场研讨会和回顾会，分析108次会议（96小时），持续30个月。

Result: 所有团队采用的TD预防策略和文档相似；团队利用各自待办事项并创建新的TD待办事项类型，包含利息、传染性、重新提交日期、讨论缺点和风险的提醒等相似属性；但使用不同的优先级方法和不同的偿还方法；团队在建立过程中需克服类似挑战。

Conclusion: 确定了各团队采用的TDM方法作为最佳实践起点；为挑战提供了解决方案或将其确定为研究空白；建议问题跟踪系统供应商实现采用所识别属性的TD问题类型；创建了供实践者建立TDM流程的白皮书。

Abstract: Context. Technical Debt (TD) refers to short-term beneficial software solutions that impede future changes, making TD management essential. However, establishing a TD management (TDM) process is one of the most pressing concerns in practice. Goal. We plan to identify which previously researched TDM approaches are feasible in practice and what typical challenges emerge to create a guideline for establishing a TDM process. Method. We replicated our previously published action research study by conducting five workshops introducing TDM with two teams from different companies. To determine the feasibility of TDM approaches, we presented the teams with approaches for various TD activities and let them decide which to adopt. Overall, we conducted 19 workshops and retrospectives, analyzing 108 meetings (96 hours) over a 30-month period. Results. The adopted TD prevention strategies and documentation were similar in all teams. The teams utilized their respective backlogs and created a new backlog item type for TD, incorporating similar attributes such as interest, contagiousness, a resubmission date, and reminders to discuss drawbacks and risks. However, they used different prioritization approaches and deviating repayment methods. The teams had to overcome similar challenges during the establishment, which we list in this paper. Conclusions. We identified the TDM approaches used by all teams as a starting point for best practices. For challenges, we provided solutions or identified them as research gaps. Issue tracking system vendors should implement TD issue types employing the identified attributes. Finally, we created a white paper for practitioners to establish a TDM process based on our results.

</details>


### [25] [RippleGUItester: Change-Aware Exploratory Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03121)
*Yanqi Su,Michael Pradel,Chunyang Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: RippleGUItester是一种变更驱动的GUI测试系统，利用LLM分析代码变更的影响，生成测试场景，通过比较变更前后的视觉GUI差异来检测意外bug。在4个真实系统上发现26个未知bug，表明该方法能有效补充现有测试流程，实现更早的回归检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件系统通过频繁的代码变更持续演进，但这些变更经常引入意外的bug，尽管进行了大量测试和代码审查。现有测试方法很大程度上受限于预定义执行路径或依赖无引导探索，导致许多变更引发的问题未被检测到。

Method: 提出RippleGUItester，一种变更驱动的测试系统，将代码变更视为涟漪效应的震中，通过GUI探索其更广泛的用户可见影响。具体包括：1) 基于LLM的变更影响分析生成和丰富现实测试场景；2) 在变更前后版本上执行这些场景；3) 应用差异分析识别行为差异；4) 多模态bug检测，比较视觉GUI变更并在自然语言变更意图上下文中进行解释，以区分意外bug和预期行为更新。

Result: 在四个广泛使用的软件系统（Firefox、Zelttr、JabRef和Godot）上进行评估，共发现26个之前未知的bug，仍存在于最新版本中。报告后，16个已修复，2个已确认，6个仍在讨论中，2个被标记为预期行为。

Conclusion: RippleGUItester可在代码变更合并之前或之后不久应用，实现更早的回归检测。该方法通过多模态bug检测和LLM辅助的变更影响分析，有效补充了现有测试套件、CI管道和代码审查的不足。

Abstract: Software systems evolve continuously through frequent code changes, yet such changes often introduce unintended bugs despite extensive testing and code review. Existing testing approaches are largely constrained to predefined execution paths or rely on unguided exploration, leaving many change-induced issues undetected. To address this challenge, we present RippleGUItester, a change-driven testing system that treats a code change as the epicenter of a ripple effect and explores its broader, user-visible impacts via the GUI. Given a code change, RippleGUItester performs LLM-based change-impact analysis to generate and enrich realistic test scenarios, executes these scenarios on both pre-change and post-change versions of the system, and applies differential analysis to identify behavioral differences. Crucially, RippleGUItester employs multimodal bug detection, comparing visual GUI changes and interpreting them in the context of natural-language change intents to distinguish unintended bugs from intended behavioral updates. We evaluate our approach on hundreds of real-world code changes across four widely used software systems: Firefox, Zettlr, JabRef, and Godot. Our results show that the proposed approach uncovers bugs introduced by code changes that were missed by existing test suites, CI pipelines, and code review. In total, we identify 26 previously unknown bugs that still exist in the latest versions of the evaluated systems. After reporting, 16 bugs have been fixed, 2 have been confirmed, 6 are still under discussion, and 2 were marked as intended. We envision RippleGUItester being applied before or shortly after a code change is merged, enabling earlier detection of regressions.

</details>


### [26] [Type-Aware Retrieval-Augmented Generation with Dependency Closure for Solver-Executable Industrial Optimization Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03180)
*Y. Zhong,R. Huang,M. Wang,Z. Guo,YC. Li,M. Yu,Z. Jin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种类型感知的检索增强生成(RAG)方法，通过构建领域特定的类型化知识库和知识图谱，实现对自然语言需求的可执行优化模型自动生成，解决了大语言模型在工业优化建模中产生不可编译代码的关键问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在自动生成工业优化模型时，经常产生不可编译的代码，主要原因是缺少声明、类型不一致以及依赖上下文不完整。这阻碍了大型语言模型在复杂工程优化任务中的实际部署。

Method: 提出类型感知RAG方法：1)通过解析异构源（如学术论文和求解器代码）构建领域特定的类型化知识库，将内容解析为类型化单元；2)在知识图谱中编码数学依赖关系；3)给定自然语言指令时，执行混合检索并通过图上的依赖传播计算最小依赖闭包，即求解器可执行代码所需的最小组类型化符号集合。

Result: 在两个约束密集型工业案例上验证：1)电池生产需求响应优化生成了包含需求响应激励和削峰约束的可执行模型，实现了削峰且保持盈利；2)柔性作业车间调度一致地产生了可编译模型并达到已知最优解。消融实验证实类型感知依赖闭包对避免结构幻觉和确保可执行性至关重要。

Conclusion: 类型感知依赖闭包是解决大型语言模型在复杂工程优化任务中部署的关键障碍的有效方法，能够确保生成代码的可执行性并实现跨领域的稳健泛化。

Abstract: Automated industrial optimization modeling requires reliable translation of natural-language requirements into solver-executable code. However, large language models often generate non-compilable models due to missing declarations, type inconsistencies, and incomplete dependency contexts. We propose a type-aware retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) method that enforces modeling entity types and minimal dependency closure to ensure executability. Unlike existing RAG approaches that index unstructured text, our method constructs a domain-specific typed knowledge base by parsing heterogeneous sources, such as academic papers and solver code, into typed units and encoding their mathematical dependencies in a knowledge graph. Given a natural-language instruction, it performs hybrid retrieval and computes a minimal dependency-closed context, the smallest set of typed symbols required for solver-executable code, via dependency propagation over the graph. We validate the method on two constraint-intensive industrial cases: demand response optimization in battery production and flexible job shop scheduling. In the first case, our method generates an executable model incorporating demand-response incentives and load-reduction constraints, achieving peak shaving while preserving profitability; conventional RAG baselines fail. In the second case, it consistently produces compilable models that reach known optimal solutions, demonstrating robust cross-domain generalization; baselines fail entirely. Ablation studies confirm that enforcing type-aware dependency closure is essential for avoiding structural hallucinations and ensuring executability, addressing a critical barrier to deploying large language models in complex engineering optimization tasks.

</details>
